In 2014, the play _Holler If Ya Hear Me_ , based on Shakur's lyrics, played on Broadway, but, among Broadway's worst-selling musicals in recent years, ran only six weeks.[315] In development since 2013, a Shakur biopic, _[All Eyez on Me](/wiki/All_Eyez_on_Me_\(film\) "All Eyez on Me \(film\)")_ , began filming in [Atlanta](/wiki/Atlanta "Atlanta") in December 2015.[316] It was released on June 16, 2017, on Shakur's 46th birthday,[317] albeit to generally negative reviews. 

In August 2019, a [docuseries](/wiki/Television_documentary "Television documentary") directed by [Allen Hughes](/wiki/Hughes_Brothers "Hughes Brothers"), _Outlaw: The Saga of Afeni and Tupac Shakur_ , was announced.[318]

### Awards and honors

[](/wiki/File:TupacShakurWalkofFameStar.jpg)Tupac Shakur's star on the Hollywood Walk of Fame

  * In 2002, Shakur was inducted into the Hip-Hop Hall of Fame. In 2004, Shakur was among the honorees at the first [Hip Hop Honors](/wiki/Hip_Hop_Honors "Hip Hop Honors").[319]
  * In 2006, Shakur's close friend and classmate Jada Pinkett Smith donated $1 million to their high school alma mater, the Baltimore School for the Arts, and named the new theater in his honor.[320][321] In 2021, Pinkett Smith honored Shakur's 50th birthday by releasing a never before seen poem she had received from him.[147]
  * In 2009, drawing praise, the Vatican added "[Changes](/wiki/Changes_\(Tupac_Shakur_song\) "Changes \(Tupac Shakur song\)")", a [1998 posthumous](/wiki/Greatest_Hits_\(Tupac_Shakur_album\) "Greatest Hits \(Tupac Shakur album\)") track, to its online playlist.[322] On June 23, 2010, the [Library of Congress](/wiki/Library_of_Congress "Library of Congress") added "[Dear Mama](/wiki/Dear_Mama "Dear Mama")" to the [National Recording Registry](/wiki/National_Recording_Registry "National Recording Registry"), the third rap song.[323][324]
  * In 2015, the [Grammy Museum](/wiki/Grammy_Museum_at_L.A._Live "Grammy Museum at L.A. Live") opened an exhibition dedicated to Shakur.[325] In his first year of eligibility, Shakur was inducted into the Rock and Roll Hall of Fame on April 7, 2017.[18][326][327]
  * In January 2022, the exhibition _Tupac Shakur: Wake Me When I'm Free_ opened at The Canvas at [L.A. Live](/wiki/L.A._Live "L.A. Live") in Los Angeles.[328]

[](/wiki/File:MacAuthurShakur.jpg)Section of MacArthur Boulevard named Tupac Shakur Way

  * On May 16, 2023, Oakland City Council voted to name the section of MacArthur Boulevard between Grand Avenue and Van Buren Avenue "Tupac Shakur Way".[329]
  * On June 7, 2023, Shakur received a star on the [Hollywood Walk of Fame](/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame "Hollywood Walk of Fame").[330][331][332] His half-sister, Sekyiwa "Set" Shakur, accepted the award in his honor.[333]



#### Rankings

  * 2002: _[Forbes](/wiki/Forbes "Forbes")_ magazine ranked Shakur at 10th among top-earning dead celebrities.[334]
  * 2003: MTV's viewers voted Shakur the greatest MC.[335]
  * 2005: Shakur was voted No.1 on Vibe's online poll of "Top 10 Best of All Time".[336]
  * 2006: MTV staff placed him second on its list of "The Greatest MCs Of All Time".[127]
  * 2012: _[The Source](/wiki/The_Source_\(magazine\) "The Source \(magazine\)")_ magazine ranked him No. 5 among "The Top 50 Lyricists".[337]
  * 2007: The [Rock and Roll Hall of Fame](/wiki/Rock_and_Roll_Hall_of_Fame "Rock and Roll Hall of Fame") placed _All Eyez on Me_ at No. 90 and _Me Against the World_ at No. 170.[338]
  * 2010: _[Rolling Stone](/wiki/Rolling_Stone "Rolling Stone")_ magazine placed Shakur at No. 86 among the "100 Greatest Artists".[280]
  * 2020: _All Eyez on Me_ was ranked No. 436 on _Rolling Stone_ 's list of the ["500 Greatest Albums Of All Time](/wiki/Rolling_Stone%27s_500_Greatest_Albums_of_All_Time "Rolling Stone's 500 Greatest Albums of All Time")".[339]
  * 2023: _Billboard_ ranked Shakur at number 4 of the top 50 rappers.[340]

No.]: Number
  *[b.]: born
  *[q.v.]: see also
  *[25]: Not actually given in Africanus; the number is deduced from Manetho's other copyists, as well as Africanus' own sum of years.
  *[69 years]: The correct sum should be 97; the total excluding Queen Hatshepsut is 75 years.
  *[263 years]: This is actually the sum of the first 13 kings, which amounts to 262 years. Adding the reign of the 14th king raises the number to 267 years.
  *[348 years]: The sum of the first thirteen kings is 263 years, just as Africanus. The "correct" sum of years would be 376.
  *[41 years]: Africanus gives 46 years, but this does not match the stated total.
  *[35 years]: Africanus gives 14 years, but this does not match the stated total.
  *[ 120 years]: Africanus' sum of items comes to 130 years.
  *[40 years]: Eusebius gives 25 years
  *[8 years]: Eusebius gives 12 years
  *[ 40 years]: Eusebius gives 44 years
  *[14 years]: Eusebius gives 12 years
  *[18 years]: Eusebius gives 20 years
